Abstract: Low-dose positron emission tomography (LD-PET) reduces radiation exposure but leads to poor image quality and hinders diagnostic confidence. Existing supervised LD to standard-dose (SD) PET recovery methods often fail to generalise across dose variations, while dose-agnostic supervised methods require paired LD-SD data. Current self-supervised methods, although more flexible, typically produce inferior results, including loss of anatomical details and oversmoothing pathological features. These pose major limitations for practical applications. To overcome these limitations, we propose SinoDiff, a novel self-supervised, physics-consistent diffusion framework for the recovery of PET sinograms across multiple predefined dose levels. Unlike the noise simulation in traditional diffusion methods, SinoDiff integrates the PET acquisition model into the forward diffusion process via Poisson thinning, enabling physically consistent sampling/modelling of dose-dependent count statistics. During the reverse diffusion process, SinoDiff estimates the incremental change in PET signals from predefined dose levels. Therefore, SinoDiff is a single, unified model that requires no retraining across multiple dose levels. To consider the characteristics of PET sinogram, we incorporate a frequency-domain convolution to capture long-range dependencies across projection angles and detector bins. Experiments on [18F]-FDG and [18F]-FDOPA datasets demonstrate that SinoDiff achieves competitive performance against supervised and self-supervised baselines across multiple dose levels.

</details>


### [239] [Boundary-Continuous Cross-Camera RGB Mapping via Hue-Split Model Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11548)
*Yuma Kinoshita,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于色调分割的模型树方法，用于跨相机的边界连续RGB颜色映射。通过沿色调坐标递归划分颜色空间，在每个节点存储仿射颜色校正矩阵（CCM），并引入边界连续的路径混合公式，在色调阈值处采用边界原型正则化抑制色度跳变。在Canon EOS-1Ds Mark II到EOS 20D的映射实验中，该方法在降低log-RMSE的同时保持了色调边界的连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统跨相机RGB映射采用单一全局仿射颜色校正矩阵（CCM），无法捕捉相机之间存在的色调特异性差异；而已有的色调分割方案会产生硬分割边界，导致输出图像出现伪轮廓。因此需要一种既能捕获色调差异又能保持边界连续性的映射方法。

Method: 该方法沿标量色调坐标递归划分源相机颜色空间，构建模型树，每个节点存储仿射CCM；节点CCM拟合采用对数域误差目标；为避免硬色调分割导致的伪轮廓，引入边界连续公式，将根到叶路径上所有节点CCM的对数域输出进行混合。路径混合权重在单纯形约束下优化，同时使用图表对拟合损失和定义在学习色调阈值两侧确定性边界原型对上的连续性正则化项。

Result: 在Canon EOS-1Ds Mark II到Canon EOS 20D映射的Middlebury Registered Color Checker数据集实验中，色调分割显著降低了相对于单一全局仿射CCM的log-RMSE，且提出的路径混合与边界原型正则化在两种光源和多种曝光条件下同时提升了精度并抑制了学习色调阈值处的色度间隙。

Conclusion: 色调分割模型树方法相比全局CCM能更有效地处理跨相机颜色的色调特异性差异，而边界连续的路径混合机制在保持精度的同时成功避免了色调阈值处的色彩跳变，验证了该方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: We propose a hue-split model-tree method for boundary-continuous cross-camera RGB mapping. Cross-camera RGB mapping aims to produce consistent color representations across cameras whose recorded RGB values differ due to sensor spectral sensitivities and image-signal processing pipelines. A common chart-based remedy is to estimate a single global affine color correction matrix (CCM), but such a global model cannot capture hue-specific discrepancies between cameras. To capture that behavior, we recursively partitions the source-camera color space along a scalar hue coordinate and builds an model tree that stores an affine CCM at every node. For fitting the node CCMs, we utilize a log-domain error objective. To prevent false contours that arise from hard hue splits, we further introduce a boundary-continuous formulation in which the prediction is obtained by blending the log-domain outputs of all node CCMs along the root-to-leaf path. The path-wise blending weights are optimized under a simplex constraint using both a chart-pair fitting loss and an explicit continuity regularizer defined on deterministic boundary prototype pairs placed just on either side of each learned hue threshold. We conducted an experiment on a Canon EOS-1Ds Mark II to Canon EOS 20D mapping using the Middlebury Registered Color Checker dataset. The results show that hue splitting substantially reduces log-RMSE over a single global affine CCM and that the proposed path blending with boundary prototype regularization simultaneously improves accuracy and suppresses chromaticity gaps at the learned hue thresholds across two illuminants and multiple exposure conditions.

</details>


### [240] [Alignment of Similarity-Transformed Images Based on Fourier--Mellin Transform Using Auxiliary Function Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11565)
*Shinji Yamashita,Yuma Kinoshita,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在亚像素精度下估计两幅图像之间相似变换（平移、缩放、旋转）的算法，结合对数极坐标下的傅里叶幅值谱进行缩放与旋转估计，以及基于辅助函数法的相位相关最大化进行平移估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 傅里叶-梅林配准方法在需要亚像素级精度估计时往往无法达到足够的对齐精度，因此需要一种更精确的相似变换估计方法。

Method: 采用两阶段估计流程：第一阶段通过对数极坐标下的傅里叶幅值谱估计缩放和旋转（不受平移影响）；第二阶段利用相位相关结合辅助函数法在空间域以亚像素精度估计平移。

Result: 在随机相似变换的图像对仿真实验中，该方法在缩放、旋转和平移的估计误差上均优于基于离散互相关的傅里叶-梅林配准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的集成方法能够有效提升图像相似变换估计的亚像素精度，相比传统傅里叶-梅林方法具有更低的估计误差。

Abstract: This paper proposes an algorithm for estimating the similarity transformation, namely translation, scale, and rotation, between two images with subpixel accuracy. Image registration is a fundamental technique for aligning images acquired under different viewpoints and imaging conditions, and a representative approach based on maximizing discrete cross-correlation is the Fourier--Mellin registration. However, the Fourier--Mellin approach often fails to achieve sufficient alignment accuracy when subpixel-level estimation is required. The proposed method integrates (i) scale-and-rotation estimation from the Fourier magnitude spectrum in a log-polar representation and (ii) maximization of phase-only correlation based on the auxiliary function method. This integration enables a two-stage estimation procedure: it first estimates scale and rotation without being affected by translation, and then estimates translation with subpixel precision in the spatial domain using the corrected image pair. A simulation experiment on image pairs subjected to random similarity transformations demonstrates that the proposed method reduces estimation errors in scale, rotation, and translation compared with Fourier--Mellin-based registration methods using discrete cross-correlation.

</details>


### [241] [A comparison of CNN architectures for Alzheimer's disease detection in single-view MRI scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11762)
*Hiram Zuniga,Ulises Orozco-Rosas,Kenia Picos*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基准测试，在相同的测试集划分协议下评估十种不同的CNN架构（包括ResNet、DenseNet、MobileNet、EfficientNet和VGG系列）用于阿尔茨海默病的四分类（正常、极轻度、轻度、中度痴呆）。采用两阶段迁移学习与全微调流程，在来自OASIS数据集（86,437张MRI脑扫描图像）的3,900张类平衡图像子集上训练。VGG16取得最佳性能，验证准确率0.9637，测试准确率0.9533。关键发现是所有十种架构均在区分\"正常\"到\"极轻度痴呆\"的过渡阶段时存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病目前无法治愈且是主要原因的致死疾病，早期检测对延缓疾病进展和保持生活质量至关重要。由于诊断依赖于MRI脑扫描，深度学习适用于阿尔茨海默病分类，但缺乏在统一协议下对不同CNN架构进行公平比较的基准研究。

Method: 提出了一个基准测试框架，在相同的held-out测试集划分协议下评估十种CNN架构（ResNet、DenseNet、MobileNet、EfficientNet和VGG家族）。引入两阶段迁移学习及全微调训练流程，使用从OASIS医学影像数据集（含86,437张单视图MRI脑扫描图像）中提取的3,900张类平衡子集进行训练，将阿尔茨海默病分为四类：正常、极轻度痴呆、轻度痴呆和中度痴呆。

Result: VGG16取得了最佳结果，验证准确率为0.9637，测试准确率为0.9533。一个关键发现是，所有十种架构在区分\"正常\"与\"极轻度痴呆\"阶段的过渡分类上均表现出持续性的困难。

Conclusion: 该基准测试展示了VGG16在阿尔茨海默病MRI分类中的优越性能，同时揭示了早期阶段（正常到极轻度痴呆）分类的普遍挑战，这为未来改进早期检测算法提供了明确的方向。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is a leading cause of death with no cure. Therefore, early detection is critical to slow progression and preserve quality of life. Diagnosis relies on medical history, cognitive tests, physical exams, and MRI brain scans, making deep learning suitable for Alzheimer's classification. This work proposes a benchmark that evaluates ten different convolutional neural network (CNN) architectures (including ResNet, DenseNet, MobileNet, EfficientNet, and VGG family models) under the same held-out test split protocol. A two-stage transfer learning and full fine-tuning pipeline is introduced to perform training using a class-balanced subset (3,900 images) derived from the OASIS medical imaging dataset, comprising 86,437 single-view MRI brain scans labeled into four classifications of Alzheimer's disease: Non-Demented, Very Mild Dementia, Mild Dementia, and Moderate Dementia. The best results were achieved by VGG16, with a 0.9637 validation accuracy and a 0.9533 test accuracy score. A key finding documented in this work is the difficulty of classifying the transition from Non-Demented to Very Mild Demented stages, observed consistently across all ten architectures.

</details>


### [242] [ResPCC: A Loss-Resilient Neural Point Cloud Codec over Lossy Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11845)
*Xueqin Niu,Mufan Liu,Yifan Wang,Le Yang,Jun Sun,Yiling Xu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: ResPCC是首个端到端、内在抗丢包的神经点云压缩编解码器，通过编码端的CALM模块和SCI机制、解码端的MGLR模块和DBR阶段，在5%-30%丢包率下实现比基线更优的稳定性与率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的点云压缩方法假设理想传输条件，而实际网络中丢包是常见问题，会严重破坏潜在特征，导致坐标漂移和几何退化，亟需内在抗丢包的端到端点云压缩方案。

Method: 提出ResPCC，框架对丢包率感知并自适应。编码端包含条件自适应潜在调制（CALM）模块调整潜在特征分布，以及空间-通道交织（SCI）机制将通道维数据丢失转化为空间散点式元素丢失；解码端包含掩码感知图神经网络潜在恢复（MGLR）模块和基于字典的细化（DBR）阶段来恢复受损特征并对齐规范先验。

Result: 在ShapeNet和SemanticKITTI数据集上，5%-30%丢包率场景下，ResPCC相较于基线方法在稳定性和率失真性能上均表现更优，在丢失条件下保持高重建保真度。

Conclusion: ResPCC为实际网络中的3D数据传输提供了可靠的抗丢包点云压缩解决方案，填补了神经点云压缩在非理想传输条件下鲁棒性的空白。

Abstract: Point cloud compression (PCC) is critical for efficient storage and transmission of 3D data. While recent learning-based PCC methods achieve good rate-distortion (R-D) performance, they generally rely on ideal transmission conditions. In practice, packet loss is a common issue and can severely distort latent features, causing coordinate drift and geometric degradation. To address this challenge, we present ResPCC, the first end-to-end neural point cloud codec designed to offer intrinsic resilience against data loss. Our framework is loss-rate-aware and adapts to diverse packet loss conditions. At the encoder, we introduce a Condition-Adaptive Latent Modulation (CALM) module to adjust latent feature distributions according to the perceived loss rate, as well as a Spatial-Channel Interleaving (SCI) mechanism that transforms channel-wise data extinction into spatially scattered element-wise missing patterns. At the decoder, we develop a Mask-Aware Graph-based Latent Restoration (MGLR) module, followed by a Dictionary-based Refinement (DBR) stage to recover corrupted features and align them with canonical priors. Evaluations on ShapeNet and SemanticKITTI under 5\% to 30\% packet loss rates show that ResPCC consistently delivers superior stability and R-D performance over baselines. Our framework maintains high reconstruction fidelity under lossy conditions, providing a reliable solution for 3D data transmission over practical networks. Code is available at https://github.com/starrynight314/ResPCC.

</details>


### [243] [Domain-Aware Lightweight Spectral-Grouped Convolutions for Hyperspectral Fish Freshness Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12227)
*Kazi Nabiul Alam,Pooneh Bagheri Zadeh,Akbar Sheikh-Akbari*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出SGNet（光谱分组网络），一种轻量级深度学习架构，用于高光谱成像的鱼类新鲜度预测。通过分组卷积和深度可分离空间路径分离光谱与空间特征提取，结合通道与空间双重注意力机制，在自主构建的三文鱼数据集上达到97.8%分类准确率和0.64天MAE，仅需4.75M参数，比ResNet-50和ViT减少5-18倍参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像可用于鱼类新鲜度无损检测，但现有深度学习方法未充分考虑HSI数据的独特性：光谱维度主导而非空间纹理、标签具有有序结构、训练样本数量有限。这些特性使得通用深度学习架构无法充分发挥HSI数据的优势。

Method: 提出SGNet轻量级架构，核心设计包括：(1) 使用分组卷积和深度可分离空间路径将光谱与空间特征提取分离；(2) 双重注意力机制，结合通道维度上的Squeeze-and-Excitation和空间维度上的门控机制，自适应地突出重要特征。整体网络参数量仅为4.75M。

Result: 在自建的16天冰箱存储三文鱼鱼片数据集上，SGNet达到97.8%的分类准确率和0.64天的平均绝对误差（MAE）。消融实验验证了各组件贡献，与ResNet-50和Vision Transformer相比参数减少5到18倍。

Conclusion: 领域感知的架构设计（针对HSI数据特性定制）能够在保持高精度的同时显著降低模型复杂度，支持工业场景下实时、精确的鱼类新鲜度预测。

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) offers nondestructive assessment of fish freshness by detecting biochemical alterations across spectral bands. However, conventional deep learning approaches do not fully address the particular characteristics of HSI data, such as spectral dominance over spatial textures, ordinal label structure, and a small number of training samples. We propose SGNet (Spectral-Grouped Network), a lightweight architecture that separates spectral and spatial feature extraction using grouped convolutions and a depthwise spatial pathway. A dual attention mechanism that couples channel-wise squeeze-and-excitation with spatial gating adaptively highlights informative features. SGNet achieves 97.8% classification accuracy and 0.64 days mean absolute error (MAE) with just 4.75M parameters when tested on our newly developed 16-day refrigerator-stored salmon fillet dataset. Ablation studies validate the contribution of each component, while comparisons demonstrate a five- to eighteen-fold parameter reduction relative to ResNet-50 and Vision Transformers. Our findings indicate that domain-aware design supports precise, real-time freshness prediction for industrial implementation.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.PM'></div>

# q-fin.PM [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Large Language Model-Driven Small-Capitalization Trading: Integrating Financial News Sentiment, Macroeconomic Indicators, and Technical Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12283)
*Alireza Kargarzadeh,Nariman Khaledian,Navid Parvini,Arman Khaledian*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种不确定性感知的投资组合构建方法，将LLM预测的风险（分为偶然性和认知性成分）直接输入协方差矩阵，而非视为固定或仅调整预期收益。在Russell 2000股票上，使用三种选股触发机制（纯alpha、纯beta和双通道一致）进行评测，发现分离的纯alpha和纯beta策略通常优于双通道交集策略。最强配置为纯beta + GPT-4o mini情绪 + Student-t目标 + 40天持有期 + 风险平价分配，在100bps成本下达到Sharpe 2.33。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型相比固定情感词典能从金融新闻中提取更丰富的信号，但现有研究在将LLM信号引入组合构建时，往往将组合风险视为固定值或仅调整预期收益，未充分利用模型对风险的不确定性预测。本文旨在探索一种不确定性感知的组合构建方法，将模型预测的风险直接输入协方差矩阵。

Method: 提出将模型预测的风险分解为偶然性（aleatoric）和认知性（epistemic）成分，直接输入组合分配器的协方差矩阵。在Russell 2000股票上设计了三种选股机制：纯alpha触发（隔离非宏观解释的异常股价变动）、纯beta触发（捕捉宏观指标先于个股的变动）和beta交集触发（双通道一致）。在完整持有期网格上评估Sharpe和收益表现。

Result: 分离的纯alpha和纯beta策略通常在各持有期上优于beta交集策略。1天持有期下，纯beta在低中等交易成本下有效（捕捉宏观到小盘股的领先-滞后溢出），但在100bps成本下失效。40天持有期下，纯beta因慢速宏观重定价而有效。最优配置为纯beta + GPT-4o mini情绪 + Student-t目标 + 40天持有期 + 风险平价，在100bps下达到Sharpe 2.33。

Conclusion: 选股机制和分配器的选择与情绪模型本身同等重要，甚至更重要。将公司特定信号与宏观暴露信号分离比要求两者同时触发更有信息含量。不同持有期下纯beta策略有效的原因各不相同，这为实践者提供了在不同市场条件下选择策略的指导。

Abstract: Large language models can extract richer signals from financial news than fixed sentiment lexicons, and recent work has explored feeding such signals into portfolio construction. We study an uncertainty-aware construction that feeds model-predicted risk -- decomposed into aleatoric and epistemic components -- directly into the covariance matrix of portfolio allocators, rather than treating portfolio risk as fixed or adjusting only expected returns. We evaluate the pipeline on Russell 2000 equities under three stock-selection regimes: a pure-alpha trigger that isolates abnormal stock moves not explained by macro indicators, a pure-beta trigger that captures macro-indicator moves before the stock itself fires, and a beta trigger in which both channels agree. Across the full holding-period grid, the separated pure-alpha and pure-beta legs usually dominate the beta intersection on Sharpe and return. Two horizons are especially informative. At one day, pure beta can work under low and moderate transaction costs because it captures immediate lead-lag spillovers from liquid macro and sector indicators into exposed small-cap stocks, but this advantage disappears at 100 bps when turnover and microstructure noise dominate. At 40 days, pure beta works for a different reason: slower macro repricing overtakes the firm-specific pure-alpha channel. The strongest conservative row is pure beta with GPT-4o mini sentiment, a Student-t target, a 40-day holding period, and risk parity allocation, reaching Sharpe 2.33 at 100 bps. The results suggest that stock-selection regime and allocator choice matter at least as much as the sentiment model, and that separating firm-specific and macro-exposure triggers is more informative than requiring both to fire simultaneously.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

### [245] [Regime-Gated Residual Mixture-of-Experts for Cross-Sectional Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12251)
*Junyi Ye,Gargi Vijay Borde*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出RG-ResMoE，一种基于制度门控的残差混合专家架构，用于股票波动率预测。该方法将制度信息仅用于专家路由而非直接预测，在美国和日本股票市场中均显著优于容量匹配的MLP基线，且比直接将制度信息加入输入更稳定、更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融波动率具有制度依赖性，但将制度信息直接融入神经网络会破坏训练稳定性。现有研究未明确回答制度信息应如何以及在哪里进入神经波动率预测模型。本文旨在探索制度信息的最佳整合路径。

Method: 提出RG-ResMoE架构：基础预测器从股票特征建模波动率，门控网络使用制度状态变量为残差校正进行专家路由。在滚动walk-forward评估框架下，匹配信息量、模型容量、超参数调优和随机种子，对1,027只美国股票进行5日已实现波动率预测，并在日本股票面板上独立验证。

Result: RG-ResMoE在预测准确性和训练稳定性上均优于容量匹配的MLP，日本市场验证了同样的增益。将制度变量直接追加到预测输入会损害性能和稳定性；限制其于路由门控则提升精度和VaR校准。硬路由始终劣于软路由。

Conclusion: 在紧凑型神经波动率预测模型中，混合专家模型的主要价值不在于增加模型容量，而在于控制非平稳制度信息如何影响预测。制度信息应通过门控路由进入模型，而非直接作为预测输入。

Abstract: Financial volatility is regime dependent, yet incorporating regime information into neural networks can also destabilize training. This paper asks where such information should enter a neural cross-sectional volatility forecasting model. We study five-day realized-volatility forecasts for 1,027 U.S. equities using a rolling walk-forward evaluation framework in which information, model capacity, hyperparameter tuning, and random seeds are matched across architectures. We propose RG-ResMoE, a regime-gated residual mixture-of-experts architecture in which regime information is used only for expert routing rather than for direct forecasting. The base predictor models volatility from stock features, while a gating network uses regime state variables to route residual corrections. RG-ResMoE consistently outperforms a capacity-matched MLP in both forecasting accuracy and training stability in the main U.S. study. Similar gains are observed on an independent Japanese panel. The integration pathway is decisive: appending the same regime variables directly to the forecasting input degrades both predictive performance and training stability, whereas restricting them to the routing gate improves accuracy and Value-at-Risk calibration. Hard routing consistently underperforms soft routing. The results suggest that, in compact neural volatility forecasting models, the primary value of mixture-of-experts models lies less in increasing model capacity than in controlling how nonstationary regime information influences prediction.

</details>
